So column 0 has strings, column 1 has numbers. We can implement: for each column i, collect values r[i] for r in rows. If all values are instances of (int, float), then return sum of them. That means we need to modify the last row's values to include \"TOTAL\" as the first column.\n\nSo render_rows function should accept mark_last=False. If mark_last is True and there is at least one row, then for the last row, we insert \"TOTAL\" at the beginning of the row's values before joining.\n\nImplementation:\n\n```python\ndef render_rows(rows, mark_last=False):\n    rendered = [\",\".join(str(v) for v in r) for r in rows]\n    if mark_last and rows:\n        # Modify the last rendered row\n        last = rendered[-1].split(\",\")\n        last.insert(0, \"TOTAL\")\n        rendered[-1] = \",\".join(last)\n    return rendered\n```\n\nBut note: if rows is empty, nothing to do.\n\nAlternatively, we could construct the last row differently, but this is fine.\n\nNow export.py: We need to handle totals parameter.
